Current thm-maximal-diagonalizable-subgroups-of-trigonalizable-groups-are-conjugate Proof2.1 does use tangent spaces: a section of the vector-group torsor f:V to Q gives surjective df_0 by degree-one terms, and its scheme kernel identifies ker(df_0) with Lie(N). That exactness is established locally in the paragraph, not imported as an unproved general right-exact Lie functor. Taking invariant tangent spaces is justified by the directly declared lem-representations-of-diagonalizable-groups-are-sums-of-eigenspaces (same batch18), whose Statement and Proof3.1 make the weight decomposition compatible with subrepresentations/quotients/extensions; the weight-zero component is therefore exact even for nonsmooth diagonalizable D. The paragraph explicitly presents graph equations y_i-P_i(x) and an invertible full-target-rank x-Jacobian minor (including the empty minor for zero target dimension), so the existing published thm-jacobian-criterion-smooth-morphism on flat-smooth-and-etale-morphisms (plan order366.073) supplies the required smoothness under AC and finite presentation; thm-flat-finite-presentation-is-open supplies openness. The general group Lie/tangent vocabulary is owned by batch14 lie-algebras-and-infinitesimal-group-schemes (order875), including def-lie-algebra-of-a-group-scheme and lem-lie-algebra-tangent-space-and-functoriality. The later batch19 lem-lie-functor-exactness-fixed-points-and-generation is not a supplier of this proof, so no later-page edge or reading-order change is introduced. Existing local proof, the exact weight and Jacobian supplier Statements/proofs, batch14/18 manifests and current plan placements were read. All qualified conjugacy/classification conclusions and their positive-characteristic boundaries remain; only a duplicate export of Herzig §§3.1-3.7 is excluded. No claim that the current structure route avoids Lie or differential input survives this corrected evidence. |

Source completeness does not make it commissioned or locally supplied; preserve all approved claims and continue its exclusion from this run. |
